<!-- CODEGRAPH_START -->
## CodeGraph

This project has a CodeGraph MCP server (`codegraph_*` tools) configured. CodeGraph is a tree-sitter-parsed knowledge graph of every symbol, edge, and file. Reads are sub-millisecond and return structural information grep cannot.

### When to prefer codegraph over native search

Use codegraph for **structural** questions: what calls what, what would break, where X is defined, what X's signature is. Use native grep/read only for **literal text** queries (string contents, comments, log messages) or after you already have a specific file open.

| Question | Tool |
|---|---|
| "Where is X defined?" / "Find symbol named X" | `codegraph_search` |
| "What calls function Y?" | `codegraph_callers` |
| "What does Y call?" | `codegraph_callees` |
| "How does X reach/become Y? / trace the flow from X to Y" | `codegraph_trace` |
| "What would break if I changed Z?" | `codegraph_impact` |
| "Show me Y's signature / source / docstring" | `codegraph_node` |
| "Give me focused context for a task/area" | `codegraph_context` |
| "See several related symbols' source at once" | `codegraph_explore` |
| "What files exist under path/" | `codegraph_files` |
| "Is the index healthy?" | `codegraph_status` |

### Rules of thumb

- Answer directly with CodeGraph for architecture, flow, and symbol questions. Start with `codegraph_context`, or `codegraph_trace` for a specific source-to-target flow, then use one `codegraph_explore` call for the relevant bodies.
- Trust CodeGraph results for indexed files. Do not re-verify them with grep unless the task is about literal text or the staleness banner says a file changed after the last sync.
- Use `codegraph_search` instead of grep when looking up a symbol by name.
- Use `codegraph_context` instead of chaining `codegraph_search` plus `codegraph_node` when you need context.
- Use `codegraph_explore` for several related symbols instead of looping many single-node reads.
- If a CodeGraph response reports pending sync or stale files, read those specific files directly; files not mentioned there are fresh.

### If `.codegraph/` doesn't exist

Ask the user whether to run `codegraph init -i` to build the index.
<!-- CODEGRAPH_END -->
</INSTRUCTIONS>

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:50:16.674Z

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:50:25.858Z

问题不是引号，是我替换 `selector` 区块的边界又切到了 `signals` 里的 `sell_signal` 之前，导致 `buy_signal` 被截成了 `day_ok anselector`。我会用补丁恢复 `signals` 区块，并保留 watchlist selector。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:50:49.449Z

我看到了 4 个文件都是同一种截断问题：`signals` 到 `selector` 的边界坏了，还残留了原 selector 的尾巴。现在我用补丁直接替换这段为完整的 `signals` 和 `watchlist selector`。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:50:58.196Z

修好了坏掉的 YAML 区块。下一步重新 dry-run；这次我会看 selector 是否确认为 `watchlist`，以及确认表达式有没有被正确保留下来。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:51:17.492Z

dry-run 全部通过，watchlist selector 和确认表达式都保留正确。现在跑完整回测；如果二次买点有效，应该看到胜率提高、平均持仓下降，但收益可能会因为少吃前段涨幅而降低，我们用实际数值判断。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:52:01.100Z

完整回测启动了，当前跑第一个二次买点版本。这个批次 4 个配置，预计一两分钟级别；我会边跑边收结果。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:52:42.375Z

前两个二次买点太稀疏了：一个只有 1 笔交易，一个 0 笔。这说明我这组确认条件比路径统计里“单票后验观察”严格很多，也可能是 watchlist 只跟踪每日 topK setup 导致样本被压得太小。先等剩下两个跑完，再放宽/修正采样方式。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:53:21.184Z

第三个也只有 3 笔，收益不是重点了，样本太少。看起来 watchlist 配置里的 `topk: 2` 同时限制了 setup 入池和确认出池，这会把观察池压得过窄；跑完第四个后我会做一组“宽 setup、窄确认”的版本。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:53:48.222Z

二次买点第一批真实结果也证实了实现细节：`topk` 会同时限制 setup 入池和确认买入，所以太窄时样本几乎没了；放松的第 4 个样本够多但质量很差。下一步我会先确认调仓层如何处理 `topk=20` 的确认列表，再做“宽观察池 + 最终仍最多 3 只持仓”的版本。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:53:59.261Z

调仓层会从确认列表里按顺序只买可用仓位，所以可以把 watchlist 的 `topk` 放宽到 10/20，让更多 setup 进入观察池，最终仍由 `max_positions=3` 控制持仓数。现在我会做一组宽观察池版本，重点验证“第 10-20 天不坏”这条。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:58:01.562Z

我继续收最后一个宽观察池结果，然后马上做汇总表。到目前为止，二次买点这个假设“有解释力”，但直接用 watchlist 交易化还没有跑出好版本。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:58:19.630Z

我会汇总四组：原冠军、强行短持仓、窄 watchlist、宽 watchlist。然后基于结果回到交易路径上找下一步，不会把这个失败批次当成结论终点。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:58:39.560Z

这张汇总很清楚：`watchlist` 的“二次确认”能把持仓周期压下来，但当前版本没有提高胜率，收益质量也不够。接下来我会专门针对“30 天内盈利兑现”的交易做 day7/day10/day12 阈值扫描，找更像短线爆发的确认条件，而不是泛泛地“不坏”。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:59:15.569Z

我会把扫描口径稍微改精确一点：不再看“原交易最终赚没赚”，而是把原买点后的第 7/10/12/15 天当作潜在二次买入日，统计从那一天再往后 10/15 天的收益机会。这样更贴近你要的 7-15 天持仓。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:59:38.781Z

扫描结果给了两个新的、比“不坏”更具体的二次买点形态：一类是 setup 后约第 20 天重新放量/收阳加速，`ret3` 和 `open-to-close` 同时转强；另一类是 setup 后约第 18 天经历一段回踩，`ret5` 和 BBI 斜率偏弱后反而更容易再启动。
我会各做两组候选，实际买入后用 10-15 天卖点，验证“晚确认、短持仓”能不能成立。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T05:59:43.784Z

我会生成下一批“晚确认短持仓”配置，重点是 age18/20 两个窗口：
1. age20 重新加速：`ret3>3.2%` 且 `open_to_close>0.9%`。
2. age18 回踩后再启动：过去 5 天回撤明显且 BBI 斜率偏弱，但仍在观察池里等重新转强。
这批更贴近你刚才说的“爆发可能在 7-15 天之后”。

I found the first genuinely promising branch in this batch: `age18_pullback_ret5_bbi_weak_recover_top20_t12` produced `+381.18%` with max drawdown `-22.05%` over 2020-2026, much healthier than the earlier forced-short-hold and generic watchlist attempts. It is still far below the original `+4294%` champion, but it validates your intuition: the useful second entry is not “still not bad after 10-15 days”; it looks more like a mid-hold pullback around day 16-20, then a late re-acceleration setup.

The other late-confirm variants were weaker:
`age18_pullback_rsvlow`: `+22.61%`, low sample.
`age20_accel_ret3_oc_rsvl`: `+16.52%`, drawdown too high.
`age20_accel_ret3_oc`: `+155.54%`, but drawdown `-54.26%`.

So the current best next direction is clear: refine the `age18 pullback recover` branch, not the age20 acceleration branch. The next concrete step is to summarize its detailed metrics from `metrics.json` and `closed_positions.json`, especially win rate and average holding days, then generate a small parameter sweep around:
`ret5 < -4.4%`, `bbi_slope5 < -0.2%`, `watchlist_pnl > -3%`, `rsv_short > 35`, with `time_stop` 10/12/15 and `take_profit` 12/15/18.

## 用户 — 2026-07-06T06:08:04.296Z

这个胜率可以啊，但为什么收益率这么小，先提升收益率，15 左右可以接受。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T06:08:20.455Z

对，这个分支的胜率已经够像“可用策略”了，收益小主要不是信号完全不行，而是两个机制把复利压住了：交易数少很多、而且卖点把大赢家截得太早。`age18_pullback` 只有 84 笔平仓、`>=20%` 的大赢家只有 1 笔；原冠军有 213 笔平仓、44 笔 `>=20%` 大赢家。现在我先沿“保留 15 天左右持仓，但放开收益上限/提高仓位利用”的方向做 sweep。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T06:08:44.474Z

我会先量化“收益小”到底来自哪里：是样本少、仓位利用低、还是卖点过早。然后直接生成一批偏收益的版本，不再优先压到 12 天，先把持仓放到 15 左右。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T06:08:54.651Z

原因很明确：这条分支不是没有胜率，而是“大赢家上限被砍掉了”。84 笔里 52 笔盈利，`>=10%` 有 22 笔，`>=15%` 有 12 笔，但 `>=20%` 只有 1 笔；所以收益率小主要是止盈/追踪/时间止损把右尾截短了。
我现在生成“放右尾、持仓仍约 15-20 天”的版本：提高止盈到 18/21/25%，时间止损 15/18/20，追踪保护略放宽。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T11:06:30.955Z

**Goal / User Target**
User wants QuantX BBI 弱转强 strategy optimized toward an “ideal strategy”:
- Total return around `20x` (`+2000%` total return)
- Average holdings `5-10` stocks, not pos1/pos3 concentration
- Win rate close to `60%`
- Max drawdown within `30%`
- Bad market dynamically reduces positions
- Strong stocks dynamically add positions
- Average holding period around `15` trading days
User explicitly rejected `pos1/t25`: too concentrated, risky, random. Search should focus on `max_positions=5-10`; more positions means more reliable.

**Environment / Constraints**
- Repo: `${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx`
- Use `quantx-backtest` skill whenever working on QuantX; read skill and relevant refs.
- Python: `conda run -n test python` or direct `${HOME}/anaconda3/envs/test/bin/python`
- `sklearn 1.7.2` is installed; no install needed.
- Generate configs under `configs/strategies/generated/`.
- Dry-run generated configs before full backtests.
- Do not delete runs/configs/data. Worktree is dirty with lots of existing generated work.
- Current sandbox is workspace-write, network restricted, approval never.
- CodeGraph is not initialized under `quantx`; use file commands.

**Key Baselines**

## Codex — 2026-07-06T11:54:35.666Z

这轮按你说的“持仓时间也要短，实盘验证不能太慢”重新跑了一批，结论比较明确：目前还没找到理想策略，但把两条路的边界摸清了。

我做了三件事：

1. 增强了 YAML 表达能力
   现在 `execution.buy.when` 能判断已持仓状态，用来写“强势股动态加仓”：
   [config_strategy.py](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/quantx/core/strategy/config_strategy.py:76)
   对应测试已补：
   [test_config_strategy.py](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/tests/strategy/test_config_strategy.py:441)

2. 跑了两批新实验
   新配置在：
   [dynamic_add_short](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_dynamic_add_short)
   [watchlist_util_search](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_watchlist_util_search)

3. 把踩坑结论写进文档
   新小节在：
   [14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/docs/development/14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md:414)

关键结论：

- 高收益骨架不能粗暴压到 15 天。
  `pos5/topK2` 基线是 `+3018.13%`、胜率 `48.48%`、回撤 `-30.61%`、平均持仓 `28.77`。
  一旦用弱势时间止损压到 `15-17` 天，收益/胜率/回撤一起崩，比如 `dyn_add_weak15_t22_pos8_topk2` 平均持仓 `16.52`，但收益只有 `+130.75%`，胜率 `32.62%`，回撤 `-56.96%`。

- 你说的 16 天/60% 胜率那条确实成立，但收益小的原因已经坐实。
  `age18` 高胜率分支：`+381.18%`、胜率 `61.90%`、回撤 `-22.05%`、平均/中位持仓 `16.44/17`。
  但平均持仓只有 `0.60` 只，平均资金投入 `34.11%`，空仓日 `59.15%`，`>=20%` 大赢家只有 `1` 笔。它不是仓位上限问题，而是确认信号太稀。

- 放宽 `age18` 同类确认可以提高利用率，但胜率和回撤会很快坏掉。
  例如 `age12_24_mild_rsv30_topk30_pos5` 平均持仓数提高到 `2.36`、资金投入约 `71%`，但胜率跌到 `48.77%`，回撤扩大到 `-49.59%`。
  相对较稳的 `age12_22_core_rsv35_pos5_tp15_t12` 平均持仓 `15.71`，收益 `+322.78%`，胜率 `52.48%`，但平均持仓数仍只有 `0.96`。

测试通过：`conda run -n test python -m pytest tests/strategy/test_config_strategy.py -q`，`26 passed`。

下一步我建议别再继续盲目缩短主策略持仓，也别继续单纯放宽 `age18`。更有价值的是专门统计主策略赚钱交易在 setup 后第 `7-15` 天爆发前的共性，找“第二类高质量短周期确认形态”。这才有机会同时把 15 天持仓、胜率和收益往一起拉。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:09:57.376Z

**Handoff Summary: QuantX BBI 弱转强 Strategy Search**

**User Goal**
Find a QuantX A-share strategy close to:
- Total return around `20x`
- Average holdings `5-10`
- Win rate near `60%`
- Max drawdown within `30%`
- Bad market dynamically reduces exposure
- Strong stocks dynamically add exposure
- Average holding time around `15` trading days

User strongly prefers `max_positions >= 3`, ideally search `5-10`. Pos1/pos3 concentration is not acceptable as final due to risk/randomness. User emphasized short average holding because live validation otherwise takes too long. User suggested clustering profitable vs losing trades to find shape-level differences and filter bad trades.

**Environment / Rules**
- Repo: `${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx`
- Must use `quantx-backtest` skill for QuantX work.
- Python env: `conda run -n test python`; direct env available but usual commands use conda.
- Company pip mirror if installing: `https://<INTERNAL_HOST>/simple`
- Do not delete runs/configs/data or revert unrelated user work.
- Generate strategies under `configs/strategies/generated/`.
- Dry-run every generated/modified config before full backtest.
- Worktree is dirty with existing generated dirs. Do not clean them.

**Current Worktree Changes**
Modified:
- `quantx/core/strategy/config_strategy.py`
  - Added buy-rule state variables: `is_holding`, `position_market_value`, and in `_buy_rule_values` exposes `holding_days`, `pnl_pct`, `peak_pnl_pct`, `drawdown_from_peak`, `position_weight`, etc. This enables YAML dynamic add-to-existing-position rules.
- `tests/strategy/test_config_strategy.py`
  - Added `test_rule_execution_buy_when_can_gate_adds_to_existing_positions`.
- `docs/development/14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md`
  - Added section “理想约束下的短持仓与动态加仓复盘”.

Untracked generated dirs include:
- `configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_dynamic_add_short/`
- `configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_watchlist_util_search/`
- Existing prior dirs: `ml_balance`, `pos1_balance`, `pos5_10_ml_quality`, `watchlist_profit_utilization`, etc.

Validation:
- `conda run -n test python -m pytest tests/strategy/test_config_strategy.py -q`
- Result: `26 passed`.

**Key Baselines**
High-return pos5 baseline:
- Config: `configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_stability_explore/bbi_short_long_base_pos5_topk2_close_strict_2020_2026_mainboard.yaml`
- Run: `full_bbi_short_long_base_pos5_topk2_close_strict_2020_2026_mainboard`
- Metrics: `+3018.13%`, MDD `-30.61%`, win `48.48%`, PF `2.50`, avg hold `28.77`, median hold `36`, avg pos `4.00`, max pos `5`, closed `328`, big20 `57`.
- This is the main high-return expansion baseline.

Pos5/topK4:
- Run: `full_bbi_short_long_base_pos5_topk4_close_strict_2020_2026_mainboard`
- `+2937.12%`, MDD `-29.68%`, win `48.33%`, avg hold `28.68`, avg pos `3.99`.

Pos8/topK2:
- `+1933.63%`, MDD `-36.62%`, win `45.26%`, avg pos `5.63`, avg hold `28.74`.

High-win short-hold watchlist baseline:
- Config: `configs/strategies/generated/stocktradebyz_mainboard_watchlist_late_confirm/bbi_short_long_watchlist_late_confirm_age18_pullback_ret5_bbi_weak_recover_top20_t12_close_strict_2020_2026_mainboard.yaml`
- Run: `20260706_140315_stocktradebyz_bbi_short_long_watchlist_late_confirm_age18_pullback_ret5_bbi_weak_recover_top20_t12_close_strict_2020_2026_mainboard`
- Metrics: `+381.18%`, MDD `-22.05%`, win `61.90%`, PF `1.95`, avg/median hold `16.44/17`, avg pos `0.60`, avg invested `34.11%`, zero-position days `59.15%`, big20 only `1`.
- Important conclusion: “16d + 60% win” works but suffers from signal scarcity/low utilization, not max position cap.

**Recent Experiments and Decisions**
1. Dynamic add + short-hold from high-return pos5 baseline:
   - Generated 10 configs in `stocktradebyz_mainboard_dynamic_add_short`.
   - All dry-runed and full-runed.
   - Result: Negative. Shortening high-return skeleton to `15-17` days destroys return/win/drawdown.
   - Examples:
     - `dyn_add_weak15_t22_pos8_topk2`: `+130.75%`, MDD `-56.96%`, win `32.62%`, avg hold `16.52`.
     - `dyn_add_weak12_t18_pos8_topk2`: `+82.83%`, MDD `-56.82%`, win `32.94%`, avg hold `15.70`.
     - `add_t25_pos8_topk2`: `+1398.95%`, MDD `-36.17%`, win `45.78%`, avg hold `28.49`.
   - Decision: Do not keep brute-force weak-time-stop compression as main path.

2. Watchlist utilization search from high-win short-hold baseline:
   - Generated 8 configs in `stocktradebyz_mainboard_watchlist_util_search`.
   - All dry-runed and full-runed.
   - Result: Raising utilization via looser confirmation rapidly hurts win/drawdown.
   - Examples:
     - `age12_22_core_rsv35_pos5_tp15_t12`: `+322.78%`, MDD `-36.99%`, win `52.48%`, avg hold `15.71`, avg pos `0.96`.
     - `age12_24_mild_rsv30_topk30_pos5_tp18_t15`: `+394.98%`, MDD `-49.59%`, win `48.77%`, avg hold `19.61`, avg pos `2.36`, avg invested `71%`.
   - Decision: max_positions not bottleneck for watchlist; confirmation frequency/quality is bottleneck.

3. Path feature analysis:
   - Created `runs/analysis_bbi_pos5_path_features.json`.
   - Based on 328 closed trades from pos5/topK2 baseline.
   - Key observation:
     - Big20 winners already show strong launch by day 10-15.
     - Big20 median `ret15d ~17.6%`, `peak15d ~24.5%`, avg hold `21.77`.
     - Losers median `ret15d ~-5.1%`, `peak15d ~1.8%`, avg hold `25.89`.
     - Top rule scan: day15 condition like `peak15d > 0.15`, `rsv_short_15d > 65`, reasonable drawdown had `n=35`, `win_rate=100%`, `big20_rate=85.7%`. This is retrospective and cannot directly be used for entry unless it becomes an add/continue rule.

4. Clustering analysis per user suggestion:
   - Created `runs/analysis_bbi_pos5_path_clusters.json`.
   - Used KMeans on path features days 3/5/7/10/12/15: returns, peak, trough, drawdown, RSV, BBI slope, ZX distance, volume, market breadth, deltas.
   - Selected `k=3`, silhouette `0.169` (weak/moderate; use as exploratory, not proof).
   - Clusters:
     - Cluster 0 “early fail / no launch”: `n=118`, win `11.0%`, big20 `0.85%`, avg final ret `-7.96%`, median hold `21`.
       - Median day7 ret `-4.64%`, day10 ret `-5.85%`, day15 ret `-6.91%`.
       - Peak by day15 only `0.59%`.
       - Dist zxdq negative and BBI slope weak/negative.
     - Cluster 1 “fast strong launch”: `n=38`, win `100%`, big20 `81.6%`, avg final ret `22.8%`, median hold `17`.
       - Median ret7 `14.1%`, ret10 `17.8%`, ret15 `24.6%`, peak15 `27.1%`.
     - Cluster 2 “normal/slow mild winner”: `n=172`, win `62.8%`, big20 `14.5%`, avg final ret `4.08%`, median hold `37`.
       - Median ret7 `1.73%`, ret10 `1.64%`, ret15 `1.94%`, peak15 `6.88%`.
   - Rule scans from cluster artifact:
     - Strong/add candidates:
       - `d15_strong_peak15_rsv65`: `n=37`, win `100%`, avg ret `22.6%`, big20 `83.8%`, bad cluster `0`.
       - `d10_strong_peak15_rsv65`: `n=30`, win `100%`, avg ret `22.5%`, big20 `80%`, bad cluster `0`.
       - `d10_recover_ret_pos_rsv55_slope_pos`: `n=116`, win `80.17%`, avg ret `10.6%`, big20 `37.1%`, bad cluster `4.3%`.
       - `d7_recover_ret_pos_rsv55_slope_pos`: `n=127`, win `74.8%`, avg ret `9.48%`, big20 `34.6%`.
     - Bad/exit candidates:
       - `d7_no_launch_peak_lt5_rsv_lt45`: `n=97`, win `23.7%`, avg ret `-4.85%`, bad cluster `64.9%`.
       - `d7_weak_ret_neg3_peak_lt3`: `n=89`, win `18.0%`, avg ret `-6.46%`, bad cluster `84.3%`.
       - `d10_no_launch_peak_lt5_rsv_lt45`: `n=75`, win `20%`, avg ret `-5.52%`, bad cluster `70.7%`.
       - `d15_weak_ret_neg3_peak_lt3`: `n=84`, win `9.5%`, avg ret `-8.43%`, bad cluster `86.9%`.
   - Interpretation:
     - Clustering supports shape-level separation. The biggest loser shape is “no launch by day7-10; peak remains under 3-5%; RSI weak; price below/near zxdq”.
     - This can translate into early exit after 7/10 days for only no-launch names, not broad time-stop.
     - Strong launch can translate into add-to-holding rules, but beware retrospective bias: the condition uses future day7/10 after initial buy, so it is valid as add/continue decision on held position, not as initial entry.

**Likely Next Steps**
1. Translate cluster findings into a few YAML configs from pos5/topK2 baseline:
   - Keep original high-return entry/exit.
   - Add early no-launch exit rules:
     - `holding_days >= 7 and peak_pnl_pct < 0.03 and pnl_pct < -0.03` maybe with `rsv_short < 45` and/or `close < zxdq`.
     - `holding_days >= 10 and peak_pnl_pct < 0.05 and rsv_short < 45`.
     - Avoid too aggressive exits that previously destroyed win rate.
   - Add strong add-to-holding:
     - `skip_if_holding: false`, `rebalance.buy_only_new_positions: false`.
     - `execution.buy.when`: `not is_holding or (holding_days >= 7/10 and pnl_pct > 0 and peak_pnl_pct > 0.10/0.15 and rsv_short > 55/65 and bbi_slope5 > 0 and position_weight < 0.28/0.35)`.
     - Keep max_positions 5/8; maybe use pos8 so add slots can be used by existing winners without buying tail candidates.
   - Try 4-6 configs only:
     - `cluster_exit_d7_no_launch_pos5_topk2`
     - `cluster_exit_d10_no_launch_pos5_topk2`
     - `cluster_add_d10_recover_pos8_topk2`
     - `cluster_exit_d7_add_d10_pos8_topk2`
     - maybe conservative d10/d15 add only.
2. Dry-run all generated configs, then full backtest.
3. Summarize against objective:
   - total return, win rate, MDD, avg/max position count, avg/median hold, big20, avg invested, zero days.
4. Update `docs/development/14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md` with clustering section and results.
5. Run tests if code changed. If only YAML/docs, dry-run/backtest suffice.

**Potential Pitfalls**
- Previous early exits like “3d/-4.5%” and “5d/-3.5%” destroyed performance. New cluster-based exits must target no-launch shape, not generic losers.
- Watchlist high-win branch is not enough because signal frequency too low.
- Dynamic market breadth hard filters and simple dynamic max positions hurt or did not help. Avoid repeating.
- Pos1/pos3 results not final acceptable.
- Strong add-to-holding support is new and tested, but no strategy using it has passed yet.
- KMeans silhouette is weak; use clusters as exploratory feature generator, not proof.
- Retrospective cluster features at day 7/10/15 are only valid for decisions at those days while holding, not for initial entry at day 0.

**Important Files / Artifacts**

```bash
conda run -n test python -m pytest tests/strategy/test_config_strategy.py -q
```

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:10:46.181Z

我读到引擎里已经有给买入规则暴露持仓状态的改动，接下来确认它在下单阶段是怎么过滤候选的。这样可以决定“加仓”究竟能不能在同一个 selector 候选里追加，而不是只改了 YAML 好看。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:11:00.227Z

我发现一个实现细节：现有 equal-weight rebalance 会先按“最大持仓数减当前持仓数”算可买名额，所以在已经满仓时，加仓候选拿不到 allocation。也就是说这轮更适合先验证“聚类弱形态退出”能不能提升胜率/缩短周期；真正强股动态加仓可能还需要再改一小段仓位分配逻辑。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:11:15.052Z

我会补一个很小的 rebalancer 行为修正：当 `buy_only_new_positions: false` 时，已持仓标的应该不受“剩余新仓位名额”为 0 的限制，否则动态加仓在满仓时天然失效。然后再跑聚类退出和加仓组合，不然实验会偏。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:11:23.309Z

我先把这个 rebalancer 的边界测试补上，再改逻辑。这个点会直接影响“强股动态加仓”的实验可信度，值得先钉牢。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:11:48.857Z

rebalancer 的修正已经落下去了，测试也补了。下一步我跑单测确认没有破坏原逻辑，然后生成 5-10 持仓范围内的聚类规则策略。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:12:19.140Z

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:13:06.883Z

我在设计加仓 YAML 时注意到 selector 不能引用持仓状态，所以加仓标的必须先被非持仓态的技术条件选出来，再交给 `buy.when` 判断“新买还是加仓”。这会让 topk/score 很敏感，我会把它作为实验变量控制住。
我准备生成 6 个配置：3 个只做聚类弱形态退出，3 个在退出基础上尝试强启动加仓。加仓版会用更宽的 selector 捕捉持仓中的强势形态，但 `buy.when` 会禁止这些宽条件直接开新仓。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:22:53.309Z

我看到了一个实际约束：独立加仓只会使用账户里的现金或同日卖出回笼资金，不能凭空加杠杆。所以这一轮我会把它和“早期无启动退出/行情弱减仓”配合测，否则 baseline 长期 90%+ 仓位时强股加仓触发了也没钱买。
我会生成 6 个小范围版本：两个只测试独立加仓是否真的有交易效果，两个把 d10 弱形态退出释放现金后再加仓，两个加入行情弱卖出/动态持仓上限。这样能区分“加仓机制无现金”与“规则本身无效”。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:44:39.821Z

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:45:18.796Z

第一条“强启动后继续追强”是明显反例：`-4.2%`、回撤 `-64.3%`，说明二次确认不能写成单纯追强 continuation，可能买在短线高潮后。等 pullback-recover 两条，看“强启动后回踩再修复”会不会好很多。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:46:00.722Z

pos8 追强也失败，回撤仍超过 `-62%`。这基本证明“看到 15% 峰值后还在强结构里直接买”不是二次买点，而是追高点。继续看回踩修复版。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:46:41.290Z

四条二次确认买点都跑完了，结果很明确：把 `peak>=15%` 直接转成买点是反例，尤其回踩修复版更差。这可能是因为它买到了“已经完成第一波主升后的回撤/分歧”，而不是原始持仓里的复利右尾。现在我抽完整指标，确认胜率和持仓期。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:47:10.619Z

这个结果背后的解释很关键：在原始持仓里，`day15 peak>=15%` 是“已经赚到第一段主升”的状态；如果把它当二次买点，等于把这 15% 的收益让掉，再去接后半段，很多票已经进入分歧/回撤期，所以胜率只有约 `39%-41%`。它更适合做“已持仓继续持有/加仓”的条件，不适合单独作为新买确认。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:48:02.342Z

我把这个反例也写进总结文档了：它回答了“能不能变成二次确认买点”这个问题，并解释为什么不行。这个结论挺值钱，后面能少走很多追高型二买的弯路。

## Codex — 2026-07-06T12:48:29.970Z

可以变成二次确认买点，但这次实测证明：`day15 peak >= 15%` 这种强启动形态直接当新买点是反例。

我生成并回测了 4 个 watchlist 二次确认版本，原始 `buy_signal` 只作为 setup，12-20 天内确认 `watchlist_peak_pnl > 0.15` 后再买。结果都很差：

- continuation 追强：收益 `-4.21% / -0.45%`，回撤约 `-63%`
- pullback-recover：收益 `-36% / -35%`，回撤约 `-78%`
- 胜率都只有约 `39%-41%`

核心原因：在原始持仓里，`day15 peak>=15%` 代表“已经吃到第一段主升”。如果第 15 天之后才买，相当于把最肥的一段让掉，再去接后半段分歧/回撤，所以不适合作为新买点。

我已经把这个结论记录到 [14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/docs/development/14_STRATEGY_SEARCH_2020_2026_RESULTS.md)。下一步更应该找“主升前 3-7 天的准备形态”，而不是等峰值收益已经到 15% 后再确认。

<!-- CODEGRAPH_START -->
## CodeGraph

This project has a CodeGraph MCP server (`codegraph_*` tools) configured. CodeGraph is a tree-sitter-parsed knowledge graph of every symbol, edge, and file. Reads are sub-millisecond and return structural information grep cannot.

### When to prefer codegraph over native search

Use codegraph for **structural** questions: what calls what, what would break, where X is defined, what X's signature is. Use native grep/read only for **literal text** queries (string contents, comments, log messages) or after you already have a specific file open.

| Question | Tool |
|---|---|
| "Where is X defined?" / "Find symbol named X" | `codegraph_search` |
| "What calls function Y?" | `codegraph_callers` |
| "What does Y call?" | `codegraph_callees` |
| "How does X reach/become Y? / trace the flow from X to Y" | `codegraph_trace` |
| "What would break if I changed Z?" | `codegraph_impact` |
| "Show me Y's signature / source / docstring" | `codegraph_node` |
| "Give me focused context for a task/area" | `codegraph_context` |
| "See several related symbols' source at once" | `codegraph_explore` |
| "What files exist under path/" | `codegraph_files` |
| "Is the index healthy?" | `codegraph_status` |

### Rules of thumb

- Answer directly with CodeGraph for architecture, flow, and symbol questions. Start with `codegraph_context`, or `codegraph_trace` for a specific source-to-target flow, then use one `codegraph_explore` call for the relevant bodies.
- Trust CodeGraph results for indexed files. Do not re-verify them with grep unless the task is about literal text or the staleness banner says a file changed after the last sync.
- Use `codegraph_search` instead of grep when looking up a symbol by name.
- Use `codegraph_context` instead of chaining `codegraph_search` plus `codegraph_node` when you need context.
- Use `codegraph_explore` for several related symbols instead of looping many single-node reads.
- If a CodeGraph response reports pending sync or stale files, read those specific files directly; files not mentioned there are fresh.

### If `.codegraph/` doesn't exist

Ask the user whether to run `codegraph init -i` to build the index.

  - “年度收益”
  - “下一交易日操作指南”

- Daily run 目录会额外落：
  - `next_session_guides.json`
  - 位置在 [pipeline.py](${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx/quantx/production/pipeline.py:162)

# AGENTS.md instructions

<INSTRUCTIONS>
这是我公司电脑的开发机，我的常用 python 环境是 conda 下的 test 环境，还有就是公司 pip 的源是https://<INTERNAL_HOST>/simple，如果使用公司以外的源速度会非常慢。这个机器的网络连接比较慢。

<!-- CODEGRAPH_START -->
## CodeGraph

This project has a CodeGraph MCP server (`codegraph_*` tools) configured. CodeGraph is a tree-sitter-parsed knowledge graph of every symbol, edge, and file. Reads are sub-millisecond and return structural information grep cannot.

### When to prefer codegraph over native search

Use codegraph for **structural** questions: what calls what, what would break, where X is defined, what X's signature is. Use native grep/read only for **literal text** queries (string contents, comments, log messages) or after you already have a specific file open.

| Question | Tool |
|---|---|
| "Where is X defined?" / "Find symbol named X" | `codegraph_search` |
| "What calls function Y?" | `codegraph_callers` |
| "What does Y call?" | `codegraph_callees` |
| "How does X reach/become Y? / trace the flow from X to Y" | `codegraph_trace` |
| "What would break if I changed Z?" | `codegraph_impact` |
| "Show me Y's signature / source / docstring" | `codegraph_node` |
| "Give me focused context for a task/area" | `codegraph_context` |
| "See several related symbols' source at once" | `codegraph_explore` |
| "What files exist under path/" | `codegraph_files` |
| "Is the index healthy?" | `codegraph_status` |

### Rules of thumb

- Answer directly with CodeGraph for architecture, flow, and symbol questions. Start with `codegraph_context`, or `codegraph_trace` for a specific source-to-target flow, then use one `codegraph_explore` call for the relevant bodies.
- Trust CodeGraph results for indexed files. Do not re-verify them with grep unless the task is about literal text or the staleness banner says a file changed after the last sync.
- Use `codegraph_search` instead of grep when looking up a symbol by name.
- Use `codegraph_context` instead of chaining `codegraph_search` plus `codegraph_node` when you need context.
- Use `codegraph_explore` for several related symbols instead of looping many single-node reads.
- If a CodeGraph response reports pending sync or stale files, read those specific files directly; files not mentioned there are fresh.

### If `.codegraph/` doesn't exist

Ask the user whether to run `codegraph init -i` to build the index.
<!-- CODEGRAPH_END -->
</INSTRUCTIONS>
<environment_context>
  <cwd>${HOME}/git/quantization</cwd>
  <shell>bash</shell>
  <current_date>2026-07-06</current_date>
  <timezone>Asia/Shanghai</timezone>
  <filesystem><workspace_roots><root>${HOME}/git/quantization</root></workspace_roots><permission_profile type="disabled"><file_system type="unrestricted" /></permission_profile></filesystem>
</environment_context>

```text
睡觉策略：收益 2.1007，胜率 0.4245，均持仓 33.91，均周期 56.61
弱转强代表：收益 29.3176，胜率 0.4833，均持仓 3.99，均周期 28.69

/api/configs -> 2 条
弱转强代表
睡觉策略
```

```text
24 passed in 4.55s
```

## Codex — 2026-07-06T16:08:30.083Z

**Handoff Summary**

**Repo / Environment**
- Repo root: `${HOME}/git/quantization/quantx`
- Must use `quantx-backtest` skill for QuantX work.
- Python env: `conda run -n test python`
- Direct python: `${HOME}/anaconda3/envs/test/bin/python`
- Web workspace: `http://127.0.0.1:8000`
- Company pip mirror: `https://<INTERNAL_HOST>/simple`
- User wants practical QuantX workspace improvements, prefers not seeing long config/run filenames in visualization/reporting.
- Do not delete `runs/`, `data/`, configs, or reset git. Worktree is dirty with many prior research/generated changes.

**Current User Request / Active Goal**
- User reported: when clicking a stock trade order, K-line page does not show the corresponding B/S period. They want:
  - The stock chart to open focused around the clicked B/S date or B/S round-trip interval.
  - Volume shown under K-line.
  - Mouse-controlled zoom in/out of K-line time window.
  - Ideally pan/drag time window too.

**Relevant Skill Instructions Read**
- `quantx-backtest/SKILL.md`
- `references/server-workspace.md`
- `references/report-artifacts.md`

**Current Progress**
1. Identified root cause:
   - Backend `read_symbol_detail()` already returns full `bars`, `trades`, `round_trips`.
   - Frontend `drawKline()` was hard-coded to `bars.slice(-260)`, so older clicked trades were outside the view.
2. Modified `quantx/server/static/workspace.html`:
   - Added `state.kline`:
     - `bars`, `trades`, `viewStart`, `viewEnd`, `focusDate`, dragging state.
   - Changed `symbolUrl(symbol, focusDate='', focusStart='', focusEnd='')` to include URL params:
     - `focus_date`
     - `focus_start`
     - `focus_end`
   - Changed `openSymbol()` to pass those through.
   - Click sources now pass focus info:
     - Trade rows pass trade `date`.
     - Closed position rows / gain-loss rows pass `entry_date` and `exit_date`.
     - Holding rows pass `last_trade_date || date`.
     - Next-session candidates pass signal date.
   - `loadSymbolDetail()` now calls:
     ```js
     initKline(detail.bars, detail.trades, {
       focusDate: qs.get('focus_date') || '',
       focusStart: qs.get('focus_start') || '',
       focusEnd: qs.get('focus_end') || '',
     });
     ```
   - `renderSymbolTrades()` rows now call `focusKlineDate(date)` inside current page.
   - `renderRoundTrips()` rows now call `focusKlineRange(entry_date, exit_date)` inside current page.
   - Replaced `drawKline(id, bars, trades)` with interactive K-line system:
     - `initKline()`

<!-- CODEGRAPH_START -->
## CodeGraph

This project has a CodeGraph MCP server (`codegraph_*` tools) configured. CodeGraph is a tree-sitter-parsed knowledge graph of every symbol, edge, and file. Reads are sub-millisecond and return structural information grep cannot.

### When to prefer codegraph over native search

Use codegraph for **structural** questions: what calls what, what would break, where X is defined, what X's signature is. Use native grep/read only for **literal text** queries (string contents, comments, log messages) or after you already have a specific file open.

| Question | Tool |
|---|---|
| "Where is X defined?" / "Find symbol named X" | `codegraph_search` |
| "What calls function Y?" | `codegraph_callers` |
| "What does Y call?" | `codegraph_callees` |
| "How does X reach/become Y? / trace the flow from X to Y" | `codegraph_trace` |
| "What would break if I changed Z?" | `codegraph_impact` |
| "Show me Y's signature / source / docstring" | `codegraph_node` |
| "Give me focused context for a task/area" | `codegraph_context` |
| "See several related symbols' source at once" | `codegraph_explore` |
| "What files exist under path/" | `codegraph_files` |
| "Is the index healthy?" | `codegraph_status` |

### Rules of thumb

- Answer directly with CodeGraph for architecture, flow, and symbol questions. Start with `codegraph_context`, or `codegraph_trace` for a specific source-to-target flow, then use one `codegraph_explore` call for the relevant bodies.
- Trust CodeGraph results for indexed files. Do not re-verify them with grep unless the task is about literal text or the staleness banner says a file changed after the last sync.
- Use `codegraph_search` instead of grep when looking up a symbol by name.
- Use `codegraph_context` instead of chaining `codegraph_search` plus `codegraph_node` when you need context.
- Use `codegraph_explore` for several related symbols instead of looping many single-node reads.
- If a CodeGraph response reports pending sync or stale files, read those specific files directly; files not mentioned there are fresh.

### If `.codegraph/` doesn't exist

Ask the user whether to run `codegraph init -i` to build the index.
<!-- CODEGRAPH_END -->
</INSTRUCTIONS>

  <shell>bash</shell>

</environment_context>
